// ============================================================================
// КТК-АНАЛИЗАТОР ЛОГА м2 — UI/ScreenLoad.cs
// Экран «Загрузка»: выбор файлов (multi-select), drag&drop, прогресс,
// счётчики (строк всего/распознано/нераспознано), сводка сессий по модулям.
// ============================================================================
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using KtkLogAnalyzer.Core;

namespace KtkLogAnalyzer.UI;

public class ScreenLoad : UserControl, IRefreshable
{
    private readonly KtkButton _btnOpen = new() { Text = "Открыть файлы журналов…", Primary = true, Width = 230, Height = 34 };
    private readonly KtkButton _btnAnalyze = new() { Text = "Анализировать", Width = 160, Height = 34, Enabled = false };
    private readonly ProgressBar _progress = new() { Height = 8, Visible = false };
    private readonly Label _lblProgress = new();
    private readonly DataGridView _grid = new();
    private readonly Label _drop = new();
    private readonly Panel _cards = new();

    public ScreenLoad()
    {
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        _drop.Text = "Перетащите файлы журналов сюда\n(поддержка UTF-8 и windows-1251, склейка строк, разделение модулей)";
        _drop.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        _drop.Height = 64;
        _drop.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleCenter;
        _drop.Font = Fonts.Ui(10f);
        _drop.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _drop.AllowDrop = true;
        _drop.DragEnter += (s, e) => { e.Effect = e.Data.GetDataPresent(DataFormats.FileDrop) ? DragDropEffects.Copy : DragDropEffects.None; };
        _drop.DragDrop += (s, e) => LoadFiles((string[])e.Data.GetData(DataFormats.FileDrop));

        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 96 };
        top.Controls.Add(_btnOpen); _btnOpen.Location = new Point(0, 0);
        top.Controls.Add(_btnAnalyze); _btnAnalyze.Location = new Point(250, 0);
        top.Controls.Add(_progress); _progress.Location = new Point(0, 46); _progress.Width = 900; _progress.Anchor = AnchorStyles.Left | AnchorStyles.Top | AnchorStyles.Right;
        top.Controls.Add(_lblProgress); _lblProgress.Location = new Point(0, 58); _lblProgress.AutoSize = true; _lblProgress.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;

        _btnOpen.Click += (s, e) =>
        {
            using var d = new OpenFileDialog { Multiselect = true, Filter = "Журналы м2|*.txt;*.log|Все файлы|*.*" };
            if (d.ShowDialog(this) == DialogResult.OK) LoadFiles(d.FileNames);
        };
        _btnAnalyze.Click += (s, e) =>
        {
            AppState.RunAnalysis();
            MainForm.Instance?.GotoFindings();
        };

        _grid.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _grid.BackgroundColor = Theme.C.Panel;
        _grid.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
        _grid.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
        _grid.ReadOnly = true;
        _grid.RowHeadersVisible = false;
        _grid.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;
        _grid.CellDoubleClick += GridCellDouble;

        var title = new SectionLabel { Text = "Загруженные прогоны (сессии по модулям)", Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 26 };

        _cards.Dock = DockStyle.Top; _cards.Height = 96;

        Controls.Add(_grid);
        Controls.Add(title);
        Controls.Add(_cards);
        Controls.Add(top);
        Controls.Add(_drop);
        Theme.Applied += () => { if (Visible) RefreshData(); };
    }

    private void GridCellDouble(object s, DataGridViewCellEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.RowIndex >= AppState.Sessions.Count) return;
        AppState.Current = AppState.Sessions[e.RowIndex];
        AppState.RunAnalysis();
        MainForm.Instance?.GotoOverview();
    }

    private void LoadFiles(string[] files)
    {
        _progress.Visible = true;
        _btnOpen.Enabled = false;
        Task.Run(() =>
        {
            var i = 0;
            foreach (var f in files)
            {
                i++;
                var fname = Path.GetFileName(f);
                var pr = Normalizer.ParseFile(f, (pct, stage) => BeginInvoke(() =>
                {
                    _progress.Value = Math.Min(99, pct);
                    _lblProgress.Text = $"{fname}: {stage}";
                }));
                BeginInvoke(() =>
                {
                    foreach (var s in pr.Sessions) if (s.LineTotal > 0 || s.Nodes.Count > 0 || s.Events.Count > 0) AppState.Sessions.Add(s);
                    if (pr.Error != "") MessageBox.Show(this, "Ошибка разбора: " + pr.Error, fname, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Error);
                });
            }
            BeginInvoke(() =>
            {
                _progress.Visible = false;
                _btnOpen.Enabled = true;
                _lblProgress.Text = files.Length == 1 ? "Готово" : $"Готово ({files.Length} файлов)";
                RefreshData();
            });
        });
    }

    public void RefreshData()
    {
        // карточки-счётчики
        _cards.Controls.Clear();
        var tot = AppState.Sessions.Sum(s => s.LineTotal);
        var unk = AppState.Sessions.Sum(s => s.LineUnknown);
        AddChip("Строк всего", tot.ToString(), "по всем модулям");
        AddChip("Распознано", tot == 0 ? "—" : Num.F((double)(tot - unk) / Math.Max(1, tot) * 100, 2) + "%", $"нераспознанных: {unk}");
        AddChip("Сессий/модулей", AppState.Sessions.Count.ToString(), "потоки разделены");
        AddChip("Узлов в печати", AppState.Sessions.Sum(s => s.Nodes.Count).ToString(), "карточки «ист.»");
        _cards.Controls[0].Location = new Point(0, 8);
        _cards.Controls[1].Location = new Point(228, 8);
        _cards.Controls[2].Location = new Point(456, 8);
        _cards.Controls[3].Location = new Point(684, 8);
        _btnAnalyze.Enabled = AppState.Current != null;

        // таблица сессий
        _grid.Rows.Clear();
        if (_grid.Columns.Count == 0)
        {
            _grid.Columns.Add("file", "Файл"); _grid.Columns.Add("mod", "Модуль");
            _grid.Columns.Add("lines", "Строк"); _grid.Columns.Add("rec", "Распознано, %");
            _grid.Columns.Add("scans", "Сканов"); _grid.Columns.Add("nodes", "Узлов");
            _grid.Columns.Add("edges", "Связей"); _grid.Columns.Add("time", "Период, c");
            _grid.Columns.Add("mode", "Режим"); _grid.Columns.Add("findings", "Находок");
        }
        foreach (var s in AppState.Sessions)
        {
            var an = s == AppState.Current ? AppState.Analysis : null;
            _grid.Rows.Add(Path.GetFileName(s.FilePath), s.Module, s.LineTotal,
                Num.F(s.RecognitionRatio * 100, 2), s.ScanCount, s.Nodes.Count, s.Edges.Count,
                Num.F(s.Time.WallSeconds, 1),
                s.Time.HasDebug ? "DEBUG" : s.Time.HasRun ? "RUN" : "—",
                an?.Findings.Count.ToString() ?? "—");
        }
        StyleGrid();
    }

    private void AddChip(string t, string v, string sub)
    {
        var c = new StatChip { Title = t, Value = v, Sub = sub, Size = new Size(220, 84) };
        _cards.Controls.Add(c);
    }

    private void StyleGrid()
    {
        var c = Theme.C;
        _grid.BackgroundColor = c.Panel;
        _grid.ForeColor = c.Text;
        _grid.GridColor = c.Border;
        _grid.EnableHeadersVisualStyles = false;
        _grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = c.Bg;
        _grid.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = c.TextDim;
        _grid.ColumnHeadersBorderStyle = DataGridViewHeaderBorderStyle.Single;
        foreach (DataGridViewRow r in _grid.Rows)
        {
            r.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = c.Panel;
            r.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = c.Text;
            r.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = c.PanelAlt;
            r.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionForeColor = c.Text;
        }
    }
}

ФАЙЛ 22 из 31: KtkLogAnalyzer/UI/ScreenOverview.cs   (Экран «Обзор»: паспорт, 6 чисел сходимости, графики, режимы; строк: 241)

// ============================================================================
// КТК-АНАЛИЗАТОР ЛОГА м2 — UI/ScreenOverview.cs
// Экран «Обзор»: паспорт прогона (редактор), 6 чисел сходимости (раздел 41),
// графики Ps Б У И / Ps У И / время циклов / кривая дефекта, таймлайн режимов.
// ============================================================================
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using KtkLogAnalyzer.Core;

namespace KtkLogAnalyzer.UI;

public class ScreenOverview : UserControl, IRefreshable
{
    private readonly FlowLayoutPanel _chips = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 100, WrapContents = false };
    private readonly LineChart _chartPs = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Title = "Сводные давления Ps (Б У И / У И), ати", XTitle = "время, c" };
    private readonly LineChart _chartConv = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Title = "Кривая сходимости: балансовый дефект Σ|Fc_A+Fc_B|, т/ч", XTitle = "время, c" };
    private readonly LineChart _chartTime = new() { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, Title = "Время цикла математики, мс", XTitle = "скан" };
    private readonly DataGridView _gridPassport = new();
    private readonly Panel _passportHost = new();
    private readonly KtkButton _btnPassport = new();
    private readonly SectionLabel _secConv = new() { Text = "Сходимость: τ / плато / развороты (шесть чисел раздела 41)" };
    private readonly SectionLabel _secPassport = new() { Text = "Паспорт прогона (раздел 40)" };
    private readonly Label _lblModes = new();

    public ScreenOverview()
    {
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        var root = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 2, RowCount = 1, BackColor = Color.Transparent };
        root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50));
        root.ColumnStyles.Add(new ColumnStyle(SizeType.Percent, 50));

        var left = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, BackColor = Color.Transparent };
        left.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, 30));   // паспорт-заголовок
        left.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, 264));  // паспорт: таблица + кнопка
        left.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, 30));   // сходимость-заголовок
        left.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 45));   // сходимость график
        left.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 45));   // режимы
        left.Controls.Add(_secPassport, 0, 0);
        left.Controls.Add(_passportHost, 0, 1);
        left.Controls.Add(_secConv, 0, 2);
        left.Controls.Add(_chartConv, 0, 3);
        left.Controls.Add(_lblModes, 0, 4);

        var right = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, BackColor = Color.Transparent };
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, 100));
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 55));
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 45));
        right.Controls.Add(_chips, 0, 0);
        right.Controls.Add(_chartPs, 0, 1);
        right.Controls.Add(_chartTime, 0, 2);

        root.Controls.Add(left, 0, 0);
        root.Controls.Add(right, 1, 0);
        Controls.Add(root);

        _gridPassport.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _gridPassport.BackgroundColor = Theme.C.Panel;
        _gridPassport.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
        _gridPassport.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
        _gridPassport.RowHeadersVisible = false;
        _gridPassport.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;
        _btnPassport.Text = "Заполнить паспорт прогона… (раздел 40)";
        _btnPassport.Primary = true;
        _btnPassport.Dock = DockStyle.Bottom;
        _btnPassport.Height = 30;
        _btnPassport.Click += (sender, e) =>
        {
            var ss = AppState.Current;
            if (ss == null) { MessageBox.Show(this, "Сначала загрузите журнал.", "Паспорт", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information); return; }
            using var d = new PassportDialog(ss);
            if (d.ShowDialog(this) == DialogResult.OK)
            {
                var pp = ss.Passport;
                pp.OperatorConfirmed = true; pp.MainC = d.MainC; pp.SkL = d.SkL;
                pp.KitVersion = d.KitVersion; pp.Scenario = d.Scenario;
                pp.LimPMin = d.LimMin; pp.LimPMax = d.LimMax;
                RefreshData();
                AppState.RunAnalysis(ss);
            }
        };
        _passportHost.Controls.Add(_gridPassport);
        _passportHost.Controls.Add(_btnPassport);
        _passportHost.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _lblModes.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _lblModes.Font = Fonts.Mono(9f);
        _lblModes.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _lblModes.Padding = new Padding(4);
        _lblModes.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.FixedSingle;

        Theme.Applied += () => { if (Visible) RefreshData(); };
    }

    public void RefreshData()
    {
        var s = AppState.Current;
        if (s == null)
        {
            _gridPassport.Rows.Clear(); _chips.Controls.Clear();
            return;
        }
        if (AppState.Analysis.Findings.Count == 0 || AppState.Analysis.SessionTitle != s.Title) AppState.RunAnalysis(s);

        // --- паспорт (авто + оператор) ---
        var p = s.Passport;
        if (_gridPassport.Columns.Count == 0)
        {
            _gridPassport.Columns.Add("k", "Поле"); _gridPassport.Columns.Add("v", "Значение");
            _gridPassport.Columns[0].FillWeight = 40; _gridPassport.Columns[1].FillWeight = 60;
        }
        _gridPassport.Rows.Clear();
        void Row(string k, string v) => _gridPassport.Rows.Add(k, v);
        Row("Файл", p.FileName);
        Row("Модули", s.Module);
        Row("Комплект / метод", $"{p.KitName} {p.KitVersion} · {p.Method} ({p.MethodTag})");
        Row("SHA-256 (КТК)", p.Sha256[..Math.Min(40, p.Sha256.Length)] + "…");
        Row("Время журнала", $"{Ms(p.FirstWallMs)} … {Ms(p.LastWallMs)} ({Num.F(s.Time.WallSeconds, 1)} c)");
        Row("Сканов / подсканов", $"{s.ScanCount} / {Math.Max(1, s.Time.SubscanTotal)} (релакс 1/{Math.Max(1, s.Time.SubscanTotal)})");
        Row("Децимация (тиков/печать)", s.Time.DecimationTicks.ToString());
        Row("AN/ZD/VS/REG", $"{Pv(p.AnCount)}/{Pv(p.ZdCount)}/{Pv(p.VsCount)}/{Pv(p.RegCount)} (арматуры/задвижки/…)");
        Row("CSV описания", (p.CsvIn == "" ? "—" : Path.GetFileName(p.CsvIn)) + " | " + (p.CsvOut == "" ? "—" : Path.GetFileName(p.CsvOut)));
        Row("MAIN_c (паспорт)", Pv2(p.MainC));
        Row("SK_L (паспорт)", Pv2(p.SkL));
        Row("LIM_P (паспорт)", double.IsNaN(p.LimPMin) || double.IsNaN(p.LimPMax) ? "—" : $"{Num.F(p.LimPMin, 2)} .. {Num.F(p.LimPMax, 2)} ати");
        Row("Сценарий", p.Scenario == "" ? "—" : p.Scenario);
        Row("Численные методы", p.IsNumeric ? "РАЗРЕШЕНЫ (паспорт подтверждён)" : "ЗАБЛОКИРОВАНЫ — заполните паспорт");
        var c = Theme.C;
        _gridPassport.BackgroundColor = c.Panel;
        _gridPassport.ForeColor = c.Text;
        _gridPassport.GridColor = c.Border;
        _gridPassport.EnableHeadersVisualStyles = false;
        _gridPassport.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.BackColor = c.Bg;
        _gridPassport.ColumnHeadersDefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = c.TextDim;
        foreach (DataGridViewRow r in _gridPassport.Rows) { r.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = c.Panel; r.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = c.Text; r.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = c.PanelAlt; }

        // --- шесть чисел сходимости ---
        var cv = s.Convergence;
        _chips.Controls.Clear();
        AddChip("Начальный дефект", cv.Series.Count > 0 ? Num.F(cv.InitialDefect, 2) + " т/ч" : "нет зеркал", "Σ|Fc_A+Fc_B| первый скан");
        AddChip("τ затухания", double.IsNaN(cv.TauSeconds) ? "н/д" : Num.F(cv.TauSeconds, 1) + " c", "экспон. регрессия lg дефекта");
        AddChip("Плато", cv.Series.Count > 0 ? Num.F(cv.Plateau, 2) + " т/ч" : "—", $"медиана последних {Settings.Current.ConvergencePlateauScans}");
        AddChip("Развороты знака", cv.SignReversals.ToString(), cv.Monotone ? "монотонно" : "НЕмонотонно");
        AddChip("Время до ε", double.IsNaN(cv.TimeToEpsSec) ? "не достигнуто" : Num.F(cv.TimeToEpsSec, 1) + " c", $"ε = {Num.F(cv.Eps, 2)} т/ч");
        AddChip("Зеркальных связей", cv.MirrorEdgeCount.ToString(), "обе стороны в печати");

        // --- графики ---
        _chartPs.Series.Clear();
        if (s.HealthBui.Count > 0) _chartPs.SetSeries("Б У И", s.HealthBui.Select(h => s.Time.ScanPeriodMsMedian * (h.Scan - 1) / 1000.0), s.HealthBui.Select(h => h.Ps), Theme.C.Chart[0]);
        if (s.HealthUi.Count > 0) _chartPs.SetSeries("У И", s.HealthUi.Select(h => s.Time.ScanPeriodMsMedian * (h.Scan - 1) / 1000.0), s.HealthUi.Select(h => h.Ps), Theme.C.Chart[1]);
        _chartPs.YTitle = "ати";
        _chartConv.Series.Clear();
        if (cv.Series.Count > 0)
        {
            _chartConv.SetSeries("дефект связей", cv.Series.Select(x => x.WallSec), cv.Series.Select(x => x.EdgeDefect), Theme.C.Red);
            _chartConv.SetSeries("дефект узлов |Fm−GTc|", cv.Series.Select(x => x.WallSec), cv.Series.Select(x => x.GlobalDefect), Theme.C.Chart[2]);
        }
        _chartTime.Series.Clear();
        if (s.Timings.Count > 0)
        {
            _chartTime.SetSeries("полный цикл", s.Timings.Select(t => (double)t.Scan), s.Timings.Select(t => t.CycleMs), Theme.C.Blue);
            _chartTime.SetSeries("релакс (подскан)", s.Timings.Select(t => (double)t.Scan), s.Timings.Select(t => t.RelaxMs), Theme.C.Chart[3]);
        }

        // --- таймлайн режимов ---
        _lblModes.Text = "Таймлайн режимов:\r\n" + string.Join("\r\n", s.Time.Segments.Select(x => $"  {x.Mode} — сканы {x.StartScan}..{x.EndScan}")) +
            (s.Time.HasDebug && s.Time.HasRun ? "\r\n(смешение DEBUG и RUN: значения между сегментами физически несравнимы)" : "");
    }

    private void AddChip(string t, string v, string sub)
    {
        var c = new StatChip { Title = t, Value = v, Sub = sub, Size = new Size(198, 88), Margin = new Padding(0, 6, 8, 0) };
        _chips.Controls.Add(c);
    }

    private static string Ms(double ms)
    {
        if (ms < 0) return "—";
        var t = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(ms);
        return $"{(int)t.TotalHours:00}:{t.Minutes:00}:{t.Seconds:00}";
    }
    private static string Pv(int? v) => v?.ToString() ?? "—";
    private static string Pv2(double v) => double.IsNaN(v) ? "— (нужно заполнить)" : Num.F(v, 3);
}

/// <summary>Диалог редактора паспорта прогона (раздел 40).</summary>
public class PassportDialog : Form
{
    public double MainC = double.NaN, SkL = double.NaN, LimMin = double.NaN, LimMax = double.NaN;
    public string KitVersion = "", Scenario = "";

    public PassportDialog(LogSession s)
    {
        Text = "Паспорт прогона";
        StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
        FormBorderStyle = FormBorderStyle.FixedDialog;
        MaximizeBox = false; MinimizeBox = false;
        ClientSize = new Size(460, 300);
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        Font = Fonts.Ui();

        var p = s.Passport;
        var l1 = new Label { Text = "Версия комплекта (19.785/19.799/19.801…)", Left = 12, Top = 14, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        var t1 = new TextBox { Left = 12, Top = 34, Width = 200, Text = p.KitVersion };
        var l2 = new Label { Text = "MAIN_c (подсканов на тик, ~31)", Left = 224, Top = 14, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        var t2 = new TextBox { Left = 224, Top = 34, Width = 100, Text = double.IsNaN(p.MainC) ? "" : Num.F(p.MainC, 0) };
        var l3 = new Label { Text = "SK_L (шаг скана, с)", Left = 336, Top = 14, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        var t3 = new TextBox { Left = 336, Top = 34, Width = 100, Text = double.IsNaN(p.SkL) ? "" : Num.F(p.SkL, 2) };
        var l4 = new Label { Text = "LIM_P_min / LIM_P_max (ати, 0 = нет)", Left = 12, Top = 64, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        var t4 = new TextBox { Left = 12, Top = 84, Width = 100, Text = double.IsNaN(p.LimPMin) ? "" : Num.F(p.LimPMin, 2) };
        var t5 = new TextBox { Left = 122, Top = 84, Width = 100, Text = double.IsNaN(p.LimPMax) ? "" : Num.F(p.LimPMax, 2) };
        var l5 = new Label { Text = "Сценарий прогона (что делали)", Left = 12, Top = 114, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        var t6 = new TextBox { Left = 12, Top = 134, Width = 424, Text = p.Scenario, Multiline = false };
        var note = new Label
        {
            Text = "Без паспорта численные методы (М1, М7, М9, М13, метрики сходимости) заблокированы —\nрежим «только структура» (раздел 40 ТЗ).",
            Left = 12, Top = 166, Width = 424, Height = 44, ForeColor = Theme.C.Warn, Font = Fonts.Ui(8.5f)
        };
        var ok = new KtkButton { Text = "Применить паспорт", Primary = true, Left = 12, Top = 222, Width = 180, Height = 32 };
        var def = new KtkButton { Text = "Типовые значения", Left = 204, Top = 222, Width = 150, Height = 32 };
        var cancel = new KtkButton { Text = "Отмена", Left = 366, Top = 222, Width = 70, Height = 32 };
        ok.Click += (o, e) =>
        {
            KitVersion = t1.Text;
            MainC = ParseD(t2.Text, 10);
            SkL = ParseD(t3.Text, 1);
            LimMin = ParseD(t4.Text, 0);
            LimMax = ParseD(t5.Text, 0);
            Scenario = t6.Text;
            DialogResult = DialogResult.OK;
        };
        def.Click += (o, e) => { t2.Text = "10"; t3.Text = "1"; t4.Text = "0"; t5.Text = "150"; t1.Text = "19.785"; };
        cancel.Click += (o, e) => DialogResult = DialogResult.Cancel;
        Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { l1, t1, l2, t2, l3, t3, l4, t4, t5, l5, t6, note, ok, def, cancel });
        AcceptButton = ok; CancelButton = cancel;
    }

    private static double ParseD(string t, double def)
    {
        if (double.TryParse(t?.Trim(), System.Globalization.NumberStyles.Float, System.Globalization.CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var v)) return v;
        return def;
    }
}

ФАЙЛ 23 из 31: KtkLogAnalyzer/UI/ScreenCards.cs   (Экран «Карточки»: узлы/связи/арматуры с подсветкой аномалий; строк: 272)

// ============================================================================
// КТК-АНАЛИЗАТОР ЛОГА м2 — UI/ScreenCards.cs
// Экран «Карточки»: дерево узлов (по типам), карточка узла (все строки дампа
// с подсветкой аномалий), карточка арматуры/связи, поиск, мини-графики,
// находки по выбранному объекту.
// ============================================================================
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using KtkLogAnalyzer.Core;

namespace KtkLogAnalyzer.UI;

public class ScreenCards : UserControl, IRefreshable
{
    private readonly TextBox _search = new();
    private readonly TreeView _tree = new();
    private readonly SplitContainer _split = new();
    private readonly Panel _card = new();
    private readonly Label _title = new();
    private readonly TableLayoutPanel _grid = new();
    private readonly LineChart _chart = new();
    private readonly ListBox _findings = new();
    private readonly ComboBox _cbModule = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _onlyAnomalies = new();

    public ScreenCards()
    {
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 44 };
        var lbl = new Label { Text = "Поиск:", Left = 10, Top = 13, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        _search.Left = 70; _search.Top = 10; _search.Width = 220;
        _search.TextChanged += (s, e) => RefreshData();
        _cbModule.Left = 310; _cbModule.Top = 10; _cbModule.Width = 130;
        _cbModule.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
        _cbModule.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) => RefreshData();
        _onlyAnomalies.Text = "только с аномалиями";
        _onlyAnomalies.Left = 452; _onlyAnomalies.Top = 12; _onlyAnomalies.AutoSize = true;
        _onlyAnomalies.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _onlyAnomalies.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => RefreshData();
        top.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { lbl, _search, _cbModule, _onlyAnomalies });

        _split.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _split.SplitterDistance = 320;
        _split.FixedPanel = FixedPanel.Panel1;

        _tree.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _tree.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
        _tree.HideSelection = false;
        _tree.AfterSelect += (s, e) => ShowObject(e.Node.Tag);

        _card.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _card.AutoScroll = true;
        _card.Padding = new Padding(8);
        _card.BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;

        var right = new TableLayoutPanel { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ColumnCount = 1, BackColor = Color.Transparent };
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Absolute, 40));
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 62));
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 22));
        right.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.Percent, 16));
        _title.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; _title.Font = Fonts.Ui(12f, true); _title.ForeColor = Theme.C.Text; _title.TextAlign = ContentAlignment.MiddleLeft;
        _grid.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; _grid.ColumnCount = 2; _grid.AutoScroll = true; _grid.BackColor = Theme.C.Panel;
        _grid.Padding = new Padding(6);
        _chart.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; _chart.Title = "Динамика узла по сканам";
        _findings.Dock = DockStyle.Fill; _findings.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None; _findings.Font = Fonts.Mono(8.75f);
        right.Controls.Add(_title, 0, 0);
        right.Controls.Add(_grid, 0, 1);
        right.Controls.Add(_chart, 0, 2);
        right.Controls.Add(_findings, 0, 3);

        _split.Panel1.Controls.Add(_tree);
        _split.Panel2.Controls.Add(right);
        Controls.Add(_split);
        Controls.Add(top);
        Theme.Applied += () => { if (Visible) RefreshData(); };
    }

    public void RefreshData()
    {
        if (AppState.Current == null) return;
        var s = AppState.Current;
        if (AppState.Analysis.Findings.Count == 0 || AppState.Analysis.SessionTitle != s.Title) AppState.RunAnalysis(s);

        if (_cbModule.Items.Count == 0)
        {
            foreach (var m in AppState.Sessions.Select(x => x.Module).Distinct()) _cbModule.Items.Add(m);
            if (_cbModule.Items.Count > 0) _cbModule.SelectedIndex = 0;
        }
        var module = _cbModule.Text;
        var filter = _search.Text.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        _tree.BeginUpdate();
        _tree.Nodes.Clear();
        if (s != null && s.Module == module)
        {
            AddGroup("Узлы расчётные (ист.)", s.Nodes.Values.OrderBy(n => n.Number), n => n.Name, n => n.Number + 1);
            AddGroup("БУИ (крайние точки)", s.Buis.Values.OrderBy(b => b.Number), b => b.Name, null);
            AddGroup("Арматуры (арм.)", s.Armatures.Values.OrderBy(a => a.Valve), a => a.Valve, null);
            AddGroup("Аппараты (насосы/печи/колонны/Т-О)", s.Apparatus.Values.OrderBy(a => a.Number), a => a.Kind + " " + a.Name, null);
            AddGroup("Связи (зеркала)", s.Edges.OrderBy(e => e.Key), e => e.Key, null);
        }
        _tree.EndUpdate();
        if (_tree.Nodes.Count > 0 && _tree.Nodes[0].Nodes.Count > 0) _tree.SelectedNode = _tree.Nodes[0].Nodes[0];
    }

    private void AddGroup<T>(string title, IEnumerable<T> items, Func<T, string> name, Func<T, int>? num)
    {
        var s = AppState.Current;
        var filter = _search.Text.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var findings = AppState.Analysis.Findings;
        var parent = new TreeNode(title) { ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        foreach (var it in items)
        {
            var n = name(it);
            if (filter != "" && !n.ToLowerInvariant().Contains(filter)) continue;
            if (_onlyAnomalies.Checked)
            {
                var key = n;
                if (!findings.Any(f => f.ObjectId == key || f.ObjectId.Contains(key))) continue;
            }
            var node = new TreeNode(n) { Tag = it };
            var fails = findings.Count(f => (f.ObjectId == n || f.ObjectId.Contains(n)) && f.Sev != Severity.Info);
            if (fails > 0)
            {
                node.ForeColor = Theme.C.Fail;
                node.Text = n + $"  ({fails})";
            }
            parent.Nodes.Add(node);
        }
        if (parent.Nodes.Count > 0) _tree.Nodes.Add(parent);
    }

    private void ShowObject(object o)
    {
        _grid.Controls.Clear();
        var s = AppState.Current;
        var findings = AppState.Analysis.Findings;
        _findings.Items.Clear();
        switch (o)
        {
            case NodeRecord n: ShowNode(n); break;
            case ArmatureRecord a: ShowArmature(a); break;
            case BuiRecord b: ShowBui(b); break;
            case EdgeRecord e: ShowEdge(e); break;
            case ApparatusRecord ap: ShowApparatus(ap); break;
        }
        var objName = o switch { NodeRecord x => x.Name, ArmatureRecord x => x.Valve, BuiRecord x => x.Name, EdgeRecord x => x.Key, ApparatusRecord x => x.Name, _ => "" };
        foreach (var f in FindingsRegistry.GroupEpisodes(findings.Where(f => f.ObjectId == objName || (o is EdgeRecord && f.ObjectId.Contains(objName))).ToList(), Settings.Current.EpisodeGapScans))
            _findings.Items.Add($"[{f.Sev.Ru()}] {f.MethodId}: {f.Message}");
    }

    private void Row(string k, string v, Color? color = null)
    {
        var lk = new Label { Text = k, Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim, Font = Fonts.Ui(8.75f), AutoSize = false, Padding = new Padding(2, 3, 4, 0) };
        var lv = new Label { Text = v, Dock = DockStyle.Fill, ForeColor = color ?? Theme.C.Text, Font = Fonts.Mono(8.75f), AutoSize = false, Padding = new Padding(0, 3, 2, 0) };
        _grid.RowStyles.Add(new RowStyle(SizeType.AutoSize));
        _grid.Controls.Add(lk, 0, _grid.RowCount);
        _grid.Controls.Add(lv, 1, _grid.RowCount);
        _grid.RowCount++;
    }

    private void ShowNode(NodeRecord n)
    {
        _title.Text = $"Узел №{n.Number} — {n.Name}   [{(n.Kind == NodeKind.Oi ? "ОИ" : "TUBE")}]  {n.ModelTag}";
        var last = n.Last; if (last == null) return;
        var st = Settings.Current;
        Row("ист. # (kerr -200)", $"P = {Num.F(last.P, 4)} ати   Tc = {Num.F(last.Tc, 2)} °C   V = {Num.F(last.V, 2)} м3   H = {Num.F(last.H, 2)} / H_0 = {Num.F(last.H0, 2)} м",
            last.P < -0.05 ? Theme.C.Fail : null);
        Row("осн: U1..U6 (kerr -201)", string.Join(" / ", last.U.Select(Num.G)),
            Math.Abs(last.USum - 1) > 0.03 ? Theme.C.Fail : null);
        Row("GTc / Fm (баланс узла)", $"{Num.F(last.Gtc, 2)} / {Num.F(last.Fm, 2)} т/ч   |G_in={Num.F(last.GIn, 2)} G_out={Num.F(last.GOut, 2)}",
            Math.Abs(last.Fm - last.Gtc) > 1.0 ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        Row("STORE G/L/W", $"{Num.F(last.StoreG, 3)} / {Num.F(last.StoreL, 3)} / {Num.F(last.StoreW, 3)}" + (last.StoreIsPreset ? "   (пресет _0i — у TUBE это норма)" : ""), last.StoreIsPreset && n.Kind == NodeKind.Oi ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        if (last.Arms.Count > 0)
            Row("k_ ARM (kerr -202)", string.Join("   ", last.Arms.Select(a => $"A{a.Index}({a.LinkNo}) {string.Join(";", a.Valves)}")),
                last.Arms.Any(a => a.AnyOpen) ? null : Theme.C.TextDim);
        foreach (var c in last.Conds)
        {
            var arm = last.Arms.FirstOrDefault(a => a.LinkNo == c.LinkNo);
            bool deadCond = Math.Abs(c.Value) < 1e-9 && arm is { AnyOpen: true };
            Row($"условия U{c.Index}({c.LinkNo}) (kerr -203)", $"{Num.F(c.Value, 2)}  <LS={Num.F(c.Ls, 1)}%>  ({(c.Inflow ? "приток" : "отток")})",
                deadCond ? Theme.C.Fail : null);
        }
        foreach (var sb in last.Stubs)
            Row($"штуцер #{sb.Index}-{sb.LinkNo} (kerr -204)", $"G {Num.F(sb.G, 1)}  L {Num.F(sb.L, 1)}  W {Num.F(sb.W, 1)}",
                Math.Abs(sb.Sum - 1) > 0.05 ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        foreach (var l in last.Links)
        {
            var nb = AppState.Current.Nodes.TryGetValue(l.Neighbor, out var nn) ? nn : null;
            var pOk = nb == null || Math.Abs(nb.Last?.P - l.P ?? 0) < 0.05;
            Row($"связь №{l.LinkNo}: {l.Neighbor} (kerr -205)", $"P={Num.F(l.P, 2)} ати  T={Num.F(l.T, 1)} °C" + (nb != null ? $"  (карточка соседа: {Num.F(nb.Last?.P ?? 0, 2)})" : "  (нет карточки в журнале)"),
                pOk ? null : Theme.C.Warn);
        }
        foreach (var g in last.Geos) Row($"геометрия {g.Neighbor} (kerr -206)", $"D {Num.F(g.D, 0)}  L {Num.F(g.L, 1)}  io {g.Io}");
        foreach (var f in last.Flows)
            Row($"расход {f.Key} (kerr -207)", Num.F(f.Value, 2) + " т/ч", Math.Abs(f.Value) > 0.05 && Math.Abs(f.Value) < 0.02 ? Theme.C.Fail : null);
        if (last.Oi != null)
        {
            var oi = last.Oi;
            Row("Для OI (kerr -208)", $"Em={Num.F(oi.Em, 2)} Em_L={Num.F(oi.EmL, 2)} L_={Num.F(oi.L, 1)}% Lw_={Num.F(oi.Lw, 2)} Ro_={Num.F(oi.Ro, 1)} Ro_L={Num.F(oi.RoL, 1)}");
            Row("UL (жидкая фаза)", string.Join(" / ", oi.UL.Select(Num.G)), Math.Abs(oi.UL.Sum() - 1) > 0.03 ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
            Row("UG (газовая фаза)", string.Join(" / ", oi.UG.Select(Num.G)), Math.Abs(oi.UG.Sum() - 1) > 0.03 ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        }
        Row("ряд по сканам", $"снимков {n.Snaps.Count}, сканы {n.Snaps[0].Scan}..{n.Snaps[^1].Scan}; режим {MethodEngine.ModeOf(AppState.Current, n.Snaps[^1].Scan)}");

        _chart.Title = $"Динамика узла {n.Name}";
        _chart.Series.Clear();
        var xs = n.Snaps.Select(x => x.Scan).Select(v => (double)v).ToList();
        _chart.SetSeries("P, ати", xs, n.SeriesOf(x => x.P), Theme.C.Blue);
        _chart.SetSeries("GTc", xs, n.SeriesOf(x => x.Gtc), Theme.C.Chart[2]);
        _chart.SetSeries("Fm", xs, n.SeriesOf(x => x.Fm), Theme.C.Red);
        _chart.XTitle = "скан";
    }

    private void ShowArmature(ArmatureRecord a)
    {
        _title.Text = $"Арматура — {a.Valve}";
        foreach (var x in a.Snaps.Take(30))
            Row($"скан {x.Scan}", $"K={Num.F(x.K, 2)}   {x.Node1}({x.Link1})={Num.F(x.P1, 2)} ати → {x.Node2}({x.Link2})={Num.F(x.P2, 2)} ати   ΔP={Num.F(x.Dp, 2)}   F={Num.F(x.F, 2)} т/ч",
                a.Snaps.All(z => Math.Abs(z.F) < 0.02) && x.K > 0.5 && Math.Abs(x.Dp) > 0.3 ? Theme.C.Fail : null);
        _chart.Title = $"Арматура {a.Valve}: проводимость и расход";
        _chart.Series.Clear();
        var xs = a.Snaps.Select(x => (double)x.Scan).ToList();
        _chart.SetSeries("K", xs, a.Snaps.Select(x => x.K), Theme.C.Blue);
        _chart.SetSeries("F, т/ч", xs, a.Snaps.Select(x => x.F), Theme.C.Red);
        _chart.XTitle = "скан";
    }

    private void ShowBui(BuiRecord b)
    {
        _title.Text = $"БУИ №{b.Number} — {b.Name}{(b.IsPip ? " [pip — вход]" : b.IsPop ? " [pop — выход]" : "")}";
        foreach (var x in b.Snaps.Take(30))
            Row($"скан {x.Scan}", $"P={Num.F(x.P, 2)} ати   Tc={Num.F(x.Tc, 2)} °C   Em={Num.F(x.Em, 2)}   ro={Num.F(x.Ro, 3)} кг/л   U: {string.Join("/", x.U.Select(v => Num.F(v, 2)))}",
                Math.Abs(x.USum - 1) > 0.03 ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        _chart.Title = $"БУИ {b.Name}: давление";
        _chart.Series.Clear();
        var xs = b.Snaps.Select(x => (double)x.Scan).ToList();
        _chart.SetSeries("P, ати", xs, b.Snaps.Select(x => x.P), Theme.C.Blue);
        _chart.XTitle = "скан";
    }

    private void ShowEdge(EdgeRecord e)
    {
        _title.Text = $"Связь — {e.Key}";
        foreach (var x in e.Snaps.Take(30))
        {
            var mirror = x.HasMirror ? $"  |U_A|={Num.F(Math.Abs(x.UAtA), 2)}  |U_B|={Num.F(Math.Abs(x.UAtB), 2)}" : "  (одна сторона)";
            Row($"скан {x.Scan}",
                $"U_A={Num.F(x.UAtA, 2)} U_B={Num.F(x.UAtB, 2)}{mirror}   Fc_A={Num.F(x.FcAtA, 2)} Fc_B={Num.F(x.FcAtB, 2)}   дефект=|Fc_A+Fc_B|={Num.F(x.FlowDefect, 2)}" +
                $"   P(A→B)={Num.F(x.POfBAtA, 2)}   {(!double.IsNaN(x.D) ? $"D={Num.F(x.D, 0)} L={Num.F(x.L, 1)} io={x.Io}" : "")}",
                x.MirrorDefect > Settings.Current.M1MirrorUEps ? Theme.C.Fail : x.FlowDefect > Settings.Current.M1FlowDefectEps ? Theme.C.Warn : null);
        }
        _chart.Title = $"Связь {e.Key}: балансовый дефект";
        _chart.Series.Clear();
        var xs = e.Snaps.Select(x => (double)x.Scan).ToList();
        _chart.SetSeries("|Fc_A+Fc_B|, т/ч", xs, e.Snaps.Select(x => x.HasMirror ? x.FlowDefect : double.NaN), Theme.C.Red);
        _chart.XTitle = "скан";
    }

    private void ShowApparatus(ApparatusRecord a)
    {
        _title.Text = $"Аппарат — {a.Kind} №{a.Number} {a.Name}";
        var last = a.Last; if (last == null) return;
        foreach (var kv in last.Values.OrderBy(k => k.Key, StringComparer.Ordinal))
            Row(kv.Key, Num.F(kv.Value, 3));
        _chart.Title = $"Аппарат {a.Name}";
        _chart.Series.Clear();
        var xs = a.Snaps.Select(x => (double)x.Scan).ToList();
        if (last.Values.ContainsKey("PI")) _chart.SetSeries("PI", xs, a.Snaps.Select(x => x.Get("PI")), Theme.C.Blue);
        if (last.Values.ContainsKey("P")) _chart.SetSeries("P", xs, a.Snaps.Select(x => x.Get("P")), Theme.C.Blue);
        _chart.XTitle = "скан";
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// КТК-АНАЛИЗАТОР ЛОГА м2 — UI/ScreenTopology.cs
// Экран «Топология»: граф смежности из строк «связи_1:», классификация
// фрагментов (цепочка/две линии/кольцо/ветвление/тупик), проверка соседей
// («есть в логе / нет в логе»), панель объекта, зум колесом.
// ============================================================================
using System.Drawing;
using System.Drawing.Drawing2D;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using KtkLogAnalyzer.Core;

namespace KtkLogAnalyzer.UI;

public class ScreenTopology : UserControl, IRefreshable
{
    private readonly GraphCanvas _canvas = new();
    private readonly Panel _info = new();
    private readonly Label _legend = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _cbMissing = new() { Text = "показать отсутствующих соседей", Checked = true };
    private readonly CheckBox _cbFrag = new() { Text = "красить по фрагментам" };

    public ScreenTopology()
    {
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 40 };
        var title = new SectionLabel { Text = "Граф смежности из строк «связи_1:»", Dock = DockStyle.Left, Width = 330 };
        _cbMissing.Left = 340; _cbMissing.Top = 10; _cbMissing.AutoSize = true; _cbMissing.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _cbFrag.Left = 520; _cbFrag.Top = 10; _cbFrag.AutoSize = true; _cbFrag.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _cbMissing.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => _canvas.Invalidate();
        _cbFrag.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => { _canvas.ColorByFragment = _cbFrag.Checked; _canvas.Invalidate(); };
        top.Controls.AddRange(new Control[] { title, _cbMissing, _cbFrag });

        var bottom = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Bottom, Height = 130 };
        var sp = new SplitContainer { Dock = DockStyle.Fill, FixedPanel = FixedPanel.Panel2, SplitterDistance = 700 };
        _canvas.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _canvas.NodeSelected += n => ShowInfo(n);
        sp.Panel1.Controls.Add(_canvas);
        _info.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _info.BackColor = Theme.C.Panel;
        _info.Padding = new Padding(8);
        _legend.Dock = DockStyle.Top;
        _legend.Height = 26;
        _legend.Font = Fonts.Ui(8.5f);
        _legend.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        sp.Panel2.Controls.Add(_legend);
        sp.Panel2.Controls.Add(_info);
        _info.BringToFront();

        bottom.Controls.Add(sp);
        Controls.Add(bottom);
        Controls.Add(top);
        Theme.Applied += () => { if (Visible) RefreshData(); };
    }

    private void ShowInfo(string node)
    {
        var s = AppState.Current;
        _info.Controls.Clear();
        int y = 6;
        void Line(string t, Color c)
        {
            var l = new Label { Text = t, AutoSize = true, Location = new Point(8, y), ForeColor = c, MaximumSize = new Size(spWidth, 0), Font = Fonts.Mono(8.75f) };
            _info.Controls.Add(l);
            y += l.PreferredHeight + 2;
        }
        var c = Theme.C;
        Line("Узел: " + node, c.Text);
        if (s.Nodes.TryGetValue(node, out var n))
        {
            Line($"тип: {(n.Kind == NodeKind.Oi ? "ОИ (разделение фаз)" : "TUBE (труба)")} · №{n.Number} · снимков {n.Snaps.Count}", c.TextDim);
            var last = n.Last;
            Line($"P={Num.F(last.P, 3)} ати  Tc={Num.F(last.Tc, 2)}  GTc={Num.F(last.Gtc, 2)}  Fm={Num.F(last.Fm, 2)}", c.Text);
            Line($"соседи: {string.Join(", ", last.Links.Select(l => l.Neighbor))}", c.TextDim);
        }
        else if (s.Buis.ContainsKey(node)) Line("БУИ (безусловная крайняя точка): давление и состав заданы", c.TextDim);
        else Line("НЕТ КАРТОЧКИ В ЖУРНАЛЕ — включите узел в LOG0 или учтите его при чтении топологии", c.Fail);
        var deg = s.Topology.Degree.TryGetValue(node, out var d) ? d : 0;
        Line($"степень (число связей): {deg}" + (deg == 1 ? "  — конец цепи (возможный тупик)" : ""), c.TextDim);
        var frag = s.Topology.Fragments.FirstOrDefault(f => f.Nodes.Contains(node));
        if (frag != null) Line($"фрагмент: {frag.ClassRu()} ({frag.Nodes.Count} узлов, длина цепи {frag.Diameter})", c.Blue);
    }

    private int spWidth = 380;
    public void RefreshData()
    {
        var s = AppState.Current;
        if (s == null) return;
        _canvas.BuildGraph(s, _cbMissing.Checked);
        _legend.Text = "  ЦЕПОЧКА — синий · ДВЕ ЛИНИИ/КОЛЬЦО — фиолетовый · ВЕТВЛЕНИЕ — оранжевый · изолирован — серый · ТУПИК — метка ○ · отсутствующий сосед — пунктир красный";
        spWidth = Math.Max(300, _info.Width - 16);
        if (_info.Controls.Count == 0) ShowInfo(s.Nodes.Keys.FirstOrDefault() ?? "—");
    }
}

/// <summary>Полотно графа: расстановка силами (Фрухтерман-Рейнгольд), зум, выбор.</summary>
public class GraphCanvas : UserControl
{
    private class GNode
    {
        public string Name = "";
        public float X, Y, Fx, Fy;
        public bool Present, Missing, DeadEnd;
        public int FragIndex = -1;
    }
    private class GEdge { public GNode A, B; }

    private readonly List<GNode> _nodes = new();
    private readonly List<GEdge> _edges = new();
    private readonly Dictionary<string, GNode> _map = new();
    private float _zoom = 1f;
    private Point _offset = new(60, 60);
    private GNode _sel;
    private bool _drag; private Point _dragStart; private Point _offsetStart;
    public bool ColorByFragment { get; set; }
    public event Action<string> NodeSelected;

    public GraphCanvas()
    {
        SetStyle(ControlStyles.AllPaintingInWmPaint | ControlStyles.OptimizedDoubleBuffer | ControlStyles.UserPaint | ControlStyles.ResizeRedraw, true);
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        Theme.Applied += () => Invalidate();
    }

    public void BuildGraph(LogSession s, bool withMissing)
    {
        _nodes.Clear(); _edges.Clear(); _map.Clear();
        var topo = s.Topology;
        var frags = topo.Fragments;
        GNode Get(string name, bool present)
        {
            if (!_map.TryGetValue(name, out var n))
            {
                n = new GNode { Name = name, X = 100 + (float)(_rnd.NextDouble() * 600), Y = 80 + (float)(_rnd.NextDouble() * 420), Present = present };
                _map[name] = n; _nodes.Add(n);
            }
            if (present) n.Present = true;
            return n;
        }
        foreach (var n in s.Nodes.Values) Get(n.Name, true);
        foreach (var b in s.Buis.Values) Get(b.Name, true);
        foreach (var e in s.Edges)
        {
            var a = Get(e.NameA, topo.PresentNames.Contains(e.NameA));
            var b = Get(e.NameB, topo.PresentNames.Contains(e.NameB));
            _edges.Add(new GEdge { A = a, B = b });
        }
        // тупики
        foreach (var n in s.Nodes.Values)
        {
            if (!topo.Degree.TryGetValue(n.Name, out var deg) || deg != 1) continue;
            var last = n.Last;
            if (last == null) continue;
            if ((last.Flows.Count == 0 || last.Flows.Values.All(v => Math.Abs(v) < 0.05)) &&
                Math.Abs(last.Gtc) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(last.Fm) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(last.GIn) < 0.05 && Math.Abs(last.GOut) < 0.05)
                if (_map.TryGetValue(n.Name, out var gn)) gn.DeadEnd = true;
        }
        // фрагменты
        for (int i = 0; i < frags.Count; i++)
            foreach (var name in frags[i].Nodes)
                if (_map.TryGetValue(name, out var gn)) { gn.FragIndex = i; }
        foreach (var n in _nodes) if (!topo.PresentNames.Contains(n.Name)) n.Missing = true;
        Layout();
        Invalidate();
    }

    private static readonly Random _rnd = new(20261128);

    /// <summary>Расстановка: пружинная модель, немного итераций — графы журнала небольшие.</summary>
    private void Layout()
    {
        if (_nodes.Count == 0) return;
        float W = Math.Max(600, Width - 120), H = Math.Max(400, Height - 100);
        for (int it = 0; it < 260; it++)
        {
            foreach (var n in _nodes) { n.Fx = 0; n.Fy = 0; }
            foreach (var e in _edges)
            {
                float dx = e.B.X - e.A.X, dy = e.B.Y - e.A.Y;
                float d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy + 1;
                float f = Math.Min(24f, 9000f / d2);
                float d = (float)Math.Sqrt(d2);
                e.A.Fx += dx / d * f; e.A.Fy += dy / d * f;
                e.B.Fx -= dx / d * f; e.B.Fy -= dy / d * f;
            }
            for (int i = 0; i < _nodes.Count; i++)
                for (int j = i + 1; j < _nodes.Count; j++)
                {
                    var a = _nodes[i]; var b = _nodes[j];
                    float dx = b.X - a.X, dy = b.Y - a.Y;
                    float d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy + 1;
                    float f = 260000f / d2;
                    float d = (float)Math.Sqrt(d2);
                    a.Fx -= dx / d * f; a.Fy -= dy / d * f;
                    b.Fx += dx / d * f; b.Fy += dy / d * f;
                }
            foreach (var n in _nodes)
            {
                n.X += Math.Max(-14, Math.Min(14, n.Fx * 0.02f));
                n.Y += Math.Max(-14, Math.Min(14, n.Fy * 0.02f));
            }
        }
        // нормализация в прямоугольник
        float minX = float.MaxValue, maxX = float.MinValue, minY = float.MaxValue, maxY = float.MinValue;
        foreach (var n in _nodes) { minX = Math.Min(minX, n.X); maxX = Math.Max(maxX, n.X); minY = Math.Min(minY, n.Y); maxY = Math.Max(maxY, n.Y); }
        float sx = W / Math.Max(1, maxX - minX), sy = H / Math.Max(1, maxY - minY);
        float sc = Math.Min(sx, sy);
        foreach (var n in _nodes) { n.X = 40 + (n.X - minX) * sc; n.Y = 30 + (n.Y - minY) * sc; }
    }

    private PointF Screen(GNode n) => new(_offset.X + n.X * _zoom, _offset.Y + n.Y * _zoom);

    protected override void OnMouseWheel(MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        _zoom = Math.Clamp(_zoom * (e.Delta > 0 ? 1.15f : 0.87f), 0.25f, 4f);
        Invalidate();
        base.OnMouseWheel(e);
    }

    protected override void OnMouseDown(MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        _drag = true; _dragStart = e.Location; _offsetStart = _offset;
        var hit = Hit(e.Location);
        if (hit != null) { _sel = hit; NodeSelected?.Invoke(hit.Name); }
        Invalidate();
        base.OnMouseDown(e);
    }
    protected override void OnMouseUp(MouseEventArgs e) { _drag = false; base.OnMouseUp(e); }
    protected override void OnMouseMove(MouseEventArgs e)
    {
        if (_drag && (e.Button & MouseButtons.Left) != 0 && _sel == null)
        {
            _offset = new Point(_offsetStart.X + e.X - _dragStart.X, _offsetStart.Y + e.Y - _dragStart.Y);
            Invalidate();
        }
        else Cursor = Hit(e.Location) != null ? Cursors.Hand : Cursors.Default;
        base.OnMouseMove(e);
    }

    private GNode Hit(Point p)
    {
        foreach (var n in _nodes)
        {
            var s = Screen(n);
            if (Math.Abs(p.X - s.X) < 7 && Math.Abs(p.Y - s.Y) < 7) return n;
        }
        return null;
    }

    protected override void OnPaint(PaintEventArgs e)
    {
        var g = e.Graphics;
        g.SmoothingMode = SmoothingMode.AntiAlias;
        var c = Theme.C;
        using (var bg = new SolidBrush(c.Bg)) g.FillRectangle(bg, ClientRectangle);
        if (_nodes.Count == 0)
        {
            TextRenderer.DrawText(g, "журнал не загружен", Fonts.Ui(10f), ClientRectangle, c.TextFaint, TextFormatFlags.HorizontalCenter | TextFormatFlags.VerticalCenter);
            return;
        }
        // рёбра
        foreach (var ed in _edges)
        {
            var a = Screen(ed.A); var b = Screen(ed.B);
            using var p = new Pen(!ed.A.Present || !ed.B.Present ? Color.FromArgb(170, c.Red) : c.BorderStrong, 1.2f)
            { DashStyle = !ed.A.Present || !ed.B.Present ? DashStyle.Dash : DashStyle.Solid };
            g.DrawLine(p, a, b);
        }
        // узлы
        foreach (var n in _nodes)
        {
            var s = Screen(n);
            var col = !n.Present ? c.Red : FragColor(n, c);
            using var b = new SolidBrush(n.DeadEnd ? c.Bg : col);
            g.FillEllipse(b, s.X - 6, s.Y - 6, 12, 12);
            using var p = new Pen(col, 1.4f);
            g.DrawEllipse(p, s.X - (n.DeadEnd ? 8 : 6), s.Y - (n.DeadEnd ? 8 : 6), (n.DeadEnd ? 8 : 6) * 2, (n.DeadEnd ? 8 : 6) * 2);
            var sz = g.MeasureString(n.Name, Fonts.Ui(7.5f));
            var tp = new PointF(s.X + 8, s.Y - 6);
            using var tb = new SolidBrush(n == _sel ? c.Text : (n.Present ? c.TextDim : c.Fail));
            g.DrawString(n.Name, Fonts.Ui(7.5f), tb, tp);
        }
        // легенда масштаба
        TextRenderer.DrawText(g, $"узлов: {_nodes.Count} · связей: {_edges.Count} · зум ×{Num.F(_zoom, 2)} (колесо — зум, тащить — панорама)",
            Fonts.Ui(8f), new Point(10, 8), c.TextFaint);
    }

    private Color FragColor(GNode n, Palette c)
    {
        if (!ColorByFragment) return n.Present ? c.BlueBright : c.Red;
        var frag = AppState.Current?.Topology.Fragments;
        if (frag == null || n.FragIndex < 0 || n.FragIndex >= frag.Count) return c.BlueBright;
        var f = frag[n.FragIndex];
        return f.Class switch
        {
            FragmentClass.Chain => c.Blue,
            FragmentClass.TwoParallelLines => Color.FromArgb(0x8B, 0x5C, 0xF6),
            FragmentClass.Ring => Color.FromArgb(0x9B, 0x59, 0xB6),
            FragmentClass.Branching => c.Warn,
            _ => c.TextFaint
        };
    }
}

ФАЙЛ 25 из 31: KtkLogAnalyzer/UI/ScreenFindings.cs   (Экран «Находки»: таблица, вердикты, экспорт CSV/HTML/PNG; строк: 181)

// ============================================================================
// КТК-АНАЛИЗАТОР ЛОГА м2 — UI/ScreenFindings.cs
// Экран «Находки»: таблица (группировка эпизодов, серьёзность, свидетели),
// вердикт владельца прямо в строке, фильтры, экспорт CSV / отчёт HTML /
// КТК-документ (подписанный) / PNG-графики.
// ============================================================================
using System.Drawing;
using System.Windows.Forms;
using KtkLogAnalyzer.Core;

namespace KtkLogAnalyzer.UI;

public class ScreenFindings : UserControl, IRefreshable
{
    private readonly DataGridView _grid = new();
    private readonly ComboBox _cbMethod = new();
    private readonly ComboBox _cbSev = new();
    private readonly TextBox _search = new();
    private readonly CheckBox _group = new() { Text = "группировать в эпизоды", Checked = true };
    private readonly KtkButton _btnCsv = new() { Text = "Экспорт CSV", Width = 130 };
    private readonly KtkButton _btnHtml = new() { Text = "КТК-документ (HTML)", Width = 160, Primary = true };
    private readonly KtkButton _btnPng = new() { Text = "Графики PNG", Width = 120 };

    public ScreenFindings()
    {
        BackColor = Theme.C.Bg;
        var top = new Panel { Dock = DockStyle.Top, Height = 46 };
        var l1 = new Label { Text = "Метод:", Left = 8, Top = 15, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        _cbMethod.Left = 64; _cbMethod.Top = 11; _cbMethod.Width = 220; _cbMethod.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
        var l2 = new Label { Text = "Серьёзность:", Left = 292, Top = 15, AutoSize = true, ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim };
        _cbSev.Left = 372; _cbSev.Top = 11; _cbSev.Width = 110; _cbSev.DropDownStyle = ComboBoxStyle.DropDownList;
        _cbSev.Items.AddRange(new object[] { "все", "ОШИБКА", "ОШИБКА+ВНИМАНИЕ" }); _cbSev.SelectedIndex = 0;
        _search.Left = 494; _search.Top = 11; _search.Width = 180;
        _group.Left = 684; _group.Top = 13; _group.AutoSize = true; _group.ForeColor = Theme.C.TextDim;
        _btnCsv.Left = 830; _btnCsv.Top = 9;
        _btnPng.Left = 968; _btnPng.Top = 9;
        _btnHtml.Left = 1096; _btnHtml.Top = 9;
        foreach (var ctrl in new Control[] { l1, _cbMethod, l2, _cbSev, _search, _group, _btnCsv, _btnPng, _btnHtml })
            top.Controls.Add(ctrl);
        _cbMethod.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) => Fill();
        _cbSev.SelectedIndexChanged += (s, e) => Fill();
        _search.TextChanged += (s, e) => Fill();
        _group.CheckedChanged += (s, e) => Fill();

        _grid.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
        _grid.BackgroundColor = Theme.C.Panel;
        _grid.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
        _grid.AllowUserToAddRows = false;
        _grid.RowHeadersVisible = false;
        _grid.AutoSizeColumnsMode = DataGridViewAutoSizeColumnsMode.Fill;
        _grid.Columns.Add("sev", "Серьёзность"); _grid.Columns["sev"].FillWeight = 10;
        _grid.Columns.Add("method", "Метод"); _grid.Columns["method"].FillWeight = 8;
        _grid.Columns.Add("obj", "Объект"); _grid.Columns["obj"].FillWeight = 16;
        _grid.Columns.Add("scans", "Сканы"); _grid.Columns["scans"].FillWeight = 8;
        _grid.Columns.Add("msg", "Сообщение"); _grid.Columns["msg"].FillWeight = 40;
        _grid.Columns.Add("wit", "Свидетели"); _grid.Columns["wit"].FillWeight = 22;
        _grid.Columns.Add("mode", "Режим"); _grid.Columns["mode"].FillWeight = 6;
        _grid.Columns.Add("verdict", "Вердикт владельца"); _grid.Columns["verdict"].FillWeight = 20;
        _grid.CellClick += GridCellClick;
        _grid.CellDoubleClick += (s, e) =>
        {
            if (e.RowIndex < 0) return;
            MainForm.Instance?.GotoCards();
        };

        _btnCsv.Click += (s, e) => ExportCsv();
        _btnHtml.Click += (s, e) => ExportKtk();
        _btnPng.Click += (s, e) => ExportPng();

        Controls.Add(_grid);
        Controls.Add(top);
        Theme.Applied += () => { if (Visible) RefreshData(); };
    }

    public void RefreshData()
    {
        if (AppState.Current == null) return;
        if (AppState.Analysis.Findings.Count == 0 || AppState.Analysis.SessionTitle != AppState.Current.Title) AppState.RunAnalysis();
        var methods = AppState.Analysis.Findings.Select(f => f.MethodId).Distinct().OrderBy(x => x).ToList();
        if (!_cbMethod.Items.Cast<string>().SequenceEqual(methods.Prepend("все")))
        {
            _cbMethod.Items.Clear();
            _cbMethod.Items.Add("все");
            foreach (var m in methods) _cbMethod.Items.Add(m);
            _cbMethod.SelectedIndex = 0;
        }
        Fill();
    }

    private void Fill()
    {
        _grid.Rows.Clear();
        var s = AppState.Current; if (s == null) return;
        var fl = AppState.Analysis.Findings;
        if (_cbMethod.Text != "" && _cbMethod.Text != "все") fl = fl.Where(f => f.MethodId == _cbMethod.Text).ToList();
        switch (_cbSev.Text)
        {
            case "ОШИБКА": fl = fl.Where(f => f.Sev == Severity.Fail).ToList(); break;
            case "ОШИБКА+ВНИМАНИЕ": fl = fl.Where(f => f.Sev != Severity.Info).ToList(); break;
        }
        var search = _search.Text.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        if (search != "") fl = fl.Where(f => (f.Message + f.ObjectId + f.TechText).ToLowerInvariant().Contains(search)).ToList();
        var rows = _group.Checked ? FindingsRegistry.GroupEpisodes(fl, Settings.Current.EpisodeGapScans) : fl.OrderBy(f => f.FirstScan).ToList();
        // сначала качество данных (раздел 46), затем по серьёзности
        rows = rows.OrderBy(f => f.MethodId.StartsWith("DQ") || f.MethodId == "PASPORT" ? 0 : 1)
                   .ThenBy(f => (int)f.Sev == 0 ? 3 : (int)f.Sev).ThenBy(f => f.FirstScan < 0 ? -1 : f.FirstScan).ToList();
        foreach (var f in rows.Take(3000))
        {
            var idx = _grid.Rows.Add(f.Sev.Ru(), f.MethodId, f.ObjectId,
                f.FirstScan < 0 ? "инит" : f.FirstScan == f.LastScan ? f.FirstScan.ToString() : $"{f.FirstScan}..{f.LastScan}",
                f.Message, string.Join(" | ", f.Witnesses.Take(3)), f.Mode, f.Verdict);
            _grid.Rows[idx].Tag = f;
        }
        foreach (DataGridViewRow r in _grid.Rows)
        {
            var sev = r.Cells[0].Value?.ToString();
            r.DefaultCellStyle.ForeColor = sev == "ОШИБКА" ? Theme.C.Fail : sev == "ВНИМАНИЕ" ? Theme.C.Warn : Theme.C.Info;
            r.DefaultCellStyle.BackColor = Theme.C.Panel;
            r.DefaultCellStyle.SelectionBackColor = Theme.C.PanelAlt;
        }
    }

    private void GridCellClick(object s, DataGridViewCellEventArgs e)
    {
        if (e.RowIndex < 0 || e.ColumnIndex != 7) return;   // колонка вердикта
        var f = _grid.Rows[e.RowIndex].Tag as Finding;
        if (f == null) return;
        using var m = new ContextMenuStrip();
        foreach (var v in new[] { "подтверждено (дефект)", "норма", "ложное срабатывание", "отложено", "(сброс)" })
            m.Items.Add(v, null, (o, args) =>
            {
                var verdict = v == "(сброс)" ? "" : v;
                var mark = f;
                mark.Verdict = verdict; mark.VerdictAt = DateTime.Now;
                var orig = AppState.Analysis.Findings.FirstOrDefault(x => x.MethodId == mark.MethodId && x.ObjectId == mark.ObjectId && x.FirstScan == mark.FirstScan);
                if (orig != null) { orig.Verdict = verdict; orig.VerdictAt = DateTime.Now; }
                AppState.Registry.Save();
                _grid.Rows[e.RowIndex].Cells[7].Value = verdict;
            });
        m.Show(Cursor.Position);
    }

    private List<Finding> CurrentEpisodes()
    {
        var fl = AppState.Analysis.Findings;
        if (_cbMethod.Text != "" && _cbMethod.Text != "все") fl = fl.Where(f => f.MethodId == _cbMethod.Text).ToList();
        var search = _search.Text.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        if (search != "") fl = fl.Where(f => (f.Message + f.ObjectId).ToLowerInvariant().Contains(search)).ToList();
        return FindingsRegistry.GroupEpisodes(fl, Settings.Current.EpisodeGapScans);
    }

    private void ExportCsv()
    {
        using var d = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "CSV|*.csv", FileName = "findings.csv" };
        if (d.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        Export.WriteAll(d.FileName, Export.FindingsCsv(AppState.Analysis.Findings));
        MessageBox.Show(this, "Экспортировано: " + d.FileName, "CSV", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }

    private void ExportKtk()
    {
        var s = AppState.Current; if (s == null) return;
        using var d = new SaveFileDialog { Filter = "КТК-документ (HTML)|*.html", FileName = "КТК_" + Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(s.FileName) + ".html" };
        if (d.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        var hyps = DiagTree.Run(AppState.Analysis.Findings, AppState.Cases.Cases);
        var roots = RootCauseEngine.Collapse(AppState.Analysis.Findings, s);
        var charts = ChartRenderer.RenderSessionCharts(s, 900, 300);
        var html = Export.BuildKtkHtml(s, AppState.Analysis, hyps, roots, charts, "1", Environment.UserName);
        File.WriteAllText(d.FileName, html);
        MessageBox.Show(this, "КТК-документ сформирован: " + d.FileName + "\n(открывается браузером, готов к печати и подписи)", "КТК", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }

    private void ExportPng()
    {
        var s = AppState.Current; if (s == null) return;
        using var d = new FolderBrowserDialog { Description = "Куда сохранить PNG-графики" };
        if (d.ShowDialog(this) != DialogResult.OK) return;
        var files = ChartRenderer.SaveCharts(s, d.SelectedPath);
        MessageBox.Show(this, "Сохранено файлов: " + files.Count + "\n" + string.Join("\n", files.Select(Path.GetFileName)), "PNG", MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Information);
    }
}

ФАЙЛ 26 из 31: KtkLogAnalyzer/UI/ScreenTraining.cs   (Экран «Обучение»: перфокарты, кейсы, вопросы, цикл качества; строк: 672)
